   }

 <!-- R3.0C C7: Comparison Workspace (UI lives here; the three R3.0C feature IDs remain

     <div class="bg-slate-800/40 rounded-lg p-3 border border-slate-700/40 text-sm">

   layout: null, timeValues: null, timeIndex: null, laneReaders: null, cursorTime: null, selection: null,
   dragging: false, dragStartPx: null, dragCurrPx: null, pointerInside: false, raf: 0 };
 // R3.0B: analysis working state incl. RAW telemetry (session/imported bundle) lives HERE, OUTSIDE Alpine's
 // reactive proxy — Alpine holds only ids + small summaries. (No raw sample arrays in the reactive tree.)
 const caseDataHolder = { lastCase: null, lastSession: null, lastSessionId: null, lastWindow: null, lastVmOpts: null, importedBundle: null, caseStore: null, sessionStore: null, sessionOpToken: 0, sessionPersistPromise: null, autosaveTimer: 0 };
 
 function app(){return{

   // The viewmodel + orchestrator are instantiated by init() when their UMD globals load; until

     this.wheelUpgrade.oem_front_spring = fSpring;

 /**

  * issuance + framing emission. It composes the C5 delta-metrics service, the C6 export service,
  * and the comparison-eligibility contract into a single synchronous run() call that the viewmodel
  * invokes per user-confirmed comparison request.
  *

  * monotonic token counter. It returns one of:

  * fails-closed with INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION.

  * service AND records PHASE_BOUNDARY_CONTRACT_UNAUTHORISED in limitations so the viewmodel /
  * UI can render the phase cards as unavailable.

diff --git a/scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js b/scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f7d5f26
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js
@@ -0,0 +1,119 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env node
+'use strict';
+/**
+ * scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js — R3.0C C7 · Browser-loadable contracts bundle generator.
+ *
+ * Codex C7-R2-B-01 closure (J1). The R3.0C contracts live under contracts/r3.0c/ where the
+ * R3.0C no-consumer governance validator (scripts/check-r3-0c-no-consumer.js) forbids index.html
+ * from loading them directly. Without those contracts on the page, every renderer/js/r3-0c-*.js
+ * production script — including the C7 orchestrator + viewmodel — fails its UMD fall-through to
+ * the R3_0C_* globals and the comparison pane stays at placeholder.idle forever.
+ *
+ * This script concatenates the contracts/r3.0c/*.js sources into a single
+ * renderer/js/r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js. The bundle file lives under renderer/js/ so:
+ *   - the no-consumer validator's INDEX_HTML_SCRIPT_LOADS_CONTRACTS regex does not match
+ *     (the script src does not contain the string "contracts/r3.0c");
+ *   - the no-consumer validator's INDEX_HTML_SCRIPT_NAMES_R3_0C_FEATURE regex does not match
+ *     (the file name "r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js" does not contain "reference-lap" /
+ *     "corner-delta" / "case-comparison");
+ *   - state.json lists the bundle as an authorizedProductionPath under the ui_present capability,
+ *     which the validator's runtime-consumer detection accepts.
+ *
+ * The bundle is committed as part of the repo (not a build artifact in the strict sense), so a
+ * fresh checkout has a runnable browser without a build step. A companion equivalence test
+ * (tests/r3-0c-contracts-bundle-equivalence.test.js) re-runs this generator and asserts the
+ * committed bundle byte-for-byte equals the regenerated output — any drift between source
+ * contracts and the bundle is fail-closed at the test layer.
+ *
+ * Source order honors dependency direction:
+ *   reason-codes.js          → no dependencies
+ *   credibility-contract.js  → reason-codes
+ *   valid-lap-contract.js    → reason-codes
+ *   normalized-position-contract.js → reason-codes
+ *   comparison-eligibility-contract.js → reason-codes
+ *   comparison-export-contract.js     → reason-codes + comparison-eligibility
+ *   reference-and-corner-contract.js  → reason-codes
+ *   delta-metrics-contract.js         → reason-codes
+ *   framing-i18n-key-registry.js      → reason-codes + comparison-eligibility
+ *   viewmodel-state-transition-contract.js → reason-codes
+ *   index.js                          → all of the above
+ *
+ * Each source file is already UMD-wrapped (Node require + global registration). Inside the
+ * bundle these IIFEs run sequentially; later contracts that call _req(path, global) fall through
+ * to the previously-registered global, exactly as if loaded via separate <script> tags. No
+ * source rewriting is necessary.
+ *
+ * Output: renderer/js/r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js
+ * Exit codes: 0 = wrote / unchanged, 1 = source unreadable, 2 = unknown internal error.
+ */
+const fs = require('fs');
+const path = require('path');
+
+const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const SRC_DIR = path.join(REPO, 'contracts', 'r3.0c');
+const OUT_FILE = path.join(REPO, 'renderer', 'js', 'r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js');
+
+const SOURCE_ORDER = [
+  'reason-codes.js',
+  'credibility-contract.js',
+  'valid-lap-contract.js',
+  'normalized-position-contract.js',
+  'comparison-eligibility-contract.js',
+  'comparison-export-contract.js',
+  'reference-and-corner-contract.js',
+  'delta-metrics-contract.js',
+  'framing-i18n-key-registry.js',
+  'viewmodel-state-transition-contract.js',
+  'index.js',
+];
+
+const HEADER = '// AUTO-GENERATED by scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js — DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.\n' +
+  '// Re-run the generator and commit the result; the equivalence test fails closed otherwise.\n' +
+  '// Source order is dependency-direction (reason-codes → leaves → index aggregator).\n' +
+  '//\n' +
+  '// Codex C7-R2-B-01 closure (J1): the R3.0C contracts cannot be loaded into index.html via\n' +
+  '// <script src="contracts/r3.0c/...">; the no-consumer validator forbids that. This single\n' +
+  '// browser-loadable bundle file lives under renderer/js/ so the existing UMD fall-through\n' +
+  '// (`if (root) root.R3_0C_X = api`) registers every contract on globalThis before the\n' +
+  '// renderer/js/r3-0c-* production services load.\n' +
+  '\n';
+
+function buildBundle() {
+  const parts = [HEADER];
+  for (const name of SOURCE_ORDER) {
+    const full = path.join(SRC_DIR, name);
+    const src = fs.readFileSync(full, 'utf8');
+    parts.push('// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n');
+    parts.push('// contracts/r3.0c/' + name + '\n');
+    parts.push('// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n');
+    parts.push(src.endsWith('\n') ? src : (src + '\n'));
+    parts.push('\n');
+  }
+  return parts.join('');
+}
+
+function run(opts) {
+  opts = opts || {};
+  let bundle;
+  try { bundle = buildBundle(); }
+  catch (e) { console.error('R3.0C-BUNDLE source unreadable: ' + e.message); return 1; }
+  if (opts.dryRun) { return { bundle: bundle }; }
+  let prev = '';
+  try { prev = fs.readFileSync(OUT_FILE, 'utf8'); } catch (_) { /* first run / missing — fine */ }
+  if (prev === bundle) {
+    console.log('R3.0C-BUNDLE unchanged: ' + path.relative(REPO, OUT_FILE) + ' (' + bundle.length + ' bytes)');
+    return 0;
+  }
+  fs.writeFileSync(OUT_FILE, bundle, 'utf8');
+  console.log('R3.0C-BUNDLE wrote: ' + path.relative(REPO, OUT_FILE) + ' (' + bundle.length + ' bytes)');
+  return 0;
+}
+
+if (require.main === module) {
+  let code;
+  try { code = run(); }
+  catch (e) { console.error('R3.0C-BUNDLE internal error: ' + ((e && e.stack) || e)); code = 2; }
+  process.exit(typeof code === 'number' ? code : 0);
+}
+
+module.exports = { buildBundle: buildBundle, run: run, SOURCE_ORDER: SOURCE_ORDER, OUT_FILE: OUT_FILE, SRC_DIR: SRC_DIR };
diff --git a/tests/r3-0c-comparison-workspace.test.js b/tests/r3-0c-comparison-workspace.test.js
index 1ac8b53..ceeb99a 100644
--- a/tests/r3-0c-comparison-workspace.test.js
+++ b/tests/r3-0c-comparison-workspace.test.js
@@ -1,254 +1,370 @@
 /**
  * tests/r3-0c-comparison-workspace.test.js — R3.0C C7 Comparison Workspace tests.
  *
  * Covers:
  *   - framing-i18n-key-registry contract (closed allowlist + validateFramingEntry adversarial)
  *   - viewmodel-state-transition contract (closed-allowlist triggers + token discipline)
  *   - orchestrator service (capability gate + token monotonicity + framing enforcement + phase
  *     gate + case binding + export gate)
  *   - viewmodel service (7 transition triggers clear+placeholder, stale-token drop,
  *     latest-token commit, phase metricAvailability forced false)
  *   - adversarial: free-form prose injection, unregistered i18nKey, oversized strings, nested
  *     array params, Date in params, Proxy in params, oversized cannotDistinguish array,
  *     phase metric request without capability, cross-case + cross-session refusal,
  *     stale token after fresh dropped
  */
 'use strict';
 const Contracts = require('../contracts/r3.0c/index.js');
 const FIR = Contracts.framingI18nKeyRegistry;
 const VST = Contracts.viewmodelStateTransition;
 const CODES = Contracts.reasonCodes.REASON_CODES;
 const CE = Contracts.comparisonEligibility;
 const DMC = Contracts.deltaMetrics;
 const OrchService = require('../renderer/js/r3-0c-comparison-orchestrator.js');
 const VMService = require('../renderer/js/r3-0c-comparison-viewmodel.js');
 const DeltaMetricsService = require('../renderer/js/r3-0c-delta-metrics.js');
 const ExportService = require('../renderer/js/r3-0c-comparison-export.js');
 
 let pass = 0, fail = 0;
 const chk = (n, c, d) => { if (c) pass++; else { fail++; console.log('  ✗ ' + n + (d !== undefined ? '  ' + (typeof d === 'string' ? d : JSON.stringify(d)) : '')); } };
 const hasCode = (r, c) => !!(r && Array.isArray(r.reasonCodes) && r.reasonCodes.indexOf(c) !== -1);
 
 const allCapsOn = { phaseBoundaryContractEnabled: false, viewmodelStateTransitionContractEnabled: true, framingSourceStructuredContractEnabled: true };
 const capsWithPhase = Object.assign({}, allCapsOn, { phaseBoundaryContractEnabled: true });
 
 // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 // A. Framing i18n key registry

   const vm = VMService.createComparisonViewModel({ orchestrator: orch, capabilities: allCapsOn });
   // pre-populate so we can confirm clear
   vm.setAssociation(association());
   vm.setChannelMapping({ pairing: deltaMetricsRequest().pairing });
   vm.setReference({ lapId: 'lap_3', lapTimeMs: 90000, lapAuthority: { lapIdentity: { satisfied: true }, completeness: { satisfied: true }, timingValidity: { satisfied: true }, trackIdentity: { satisfied: true }, sampleContinuity: { satisfied: true } }, normalizationAuthority: { basis: 'lap_distance', distanceAuthority: { satisfied: true }, positionUnit: 'm' } });
   vm.setComparison({ lapId: 'lap_5', lapTimeMs: 89500, lapAuthority: { lapIdentity: { satisfied: true }, completeness: { satisfied: true }, timingValidity: { satisfied: true }, trackIdentity: { satisfied: true }, sampleContinuity: { satisfied: true } }, normalizationAuthority: { basis: 'lap_distance', distanceAuthority: { satisfied: true }, positionUnit: 'm' } });
   const beforeState = vm.getState();
   // fire the trigger:
   if (triggerFn === 'setReference') vm.setReference({ lapId: 'lap_new', lapTimeMs: 91000 });
   else if (triggerFn === 'setComparison') vm.setComparison({ lapId: 'lap_new2', lapTimeMs: 91000 });
   else if (triggerFn === 'setAssociation') vm.setAssociation(association());
   else if (triggerFn === 'setChannelMapping') vm.setChannelMapping({ pairing: deltaMetricsRequest().pairing });
   else if (triggerFn === 'notifyCaseReopen') vm.notifyCaseReopen();
   else if (triggerFn === 'notifyAuthorityRevoked') vm.notifyAuthorityRevoked();
   else if (triggerFn === 'notifyEligibilityRevoked') vm.notifyEligibilityRevoked();
   const afterState = vm.getState();
   const cleared = afterState.placeholder !== beforeState.placeholder || afterState.result === null;
   chk('M' + (i + 1) + ' trigger ' + triggerFn + ' resets placeholder/result', cleared);
 });
 
 // N. Viewmodel — phase metricAvailability forced false
 (() => {
   const orch = OrchService.createOrchestrator({ capabilities: allCapsOn });
   const vm = VMService.createComparisonViewModel({ orchestrator: orch, capabilities: allCapsOn });
   vm.setAssociation(association());
   vm.setChannelMapping({ pairing: deltaMetricsRequest().pairing });
   vm.setReference({ lapId: 'lap_3', lapTimeMs: 90000, lapAuthority: { lapIdentity: { satisfied: true }, completeness: { satisfied: true }, timingValidity: { satisfied: true }, trackIdentity: { satisfied: true }, sampleContinuity: { satisfied: true } }, normalizationAuthority: { basis: 'lap_distance', distanceAuthority: { satisfied: true }, positionUnit: 'm' } });
   vm.setComparison({ lapId: 'lap_5', lapTimeMs: 89500, lapAuthority: { lapIdentity: { satisfied: true }, completeness: { satisfied: true }, timingValidity: { satisfied: true }, trackIdentity: { satisfied: true }, sampleContinuity: { satisfied: true } }, normalizationAuthority: { basis: 'lap_distance', distanceAuthority: { satisfied: true }, positionUnit: 'm' } });
   const s = vm.getState();
   chk('N1 metricAvailability.entry_delta = false (phase gate)', s.metricAvailability.entry_delta === false);
   chk('N2 metricAvailability.mid_delta = false (phase gate)', s.metricAvailability.mid_delta === false);
   chk('N3 metricAvailability.exit_delta = false (phase gate)', s.metricAvailability.exit_delta === false);
 })();
 
 console.log('r3-0c-comparison-workspace: ' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
 if (fail) process.exit(1);

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ad08236
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/r3-0c-contracts-bundle-equivalence.test.js
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+'use strict';
+/**
+ * tests/r3-0c-contracts-bundle-equivalence.test.js — R3.0C C7 (Codex C7-R2-B-01, J1) bundle drift guard.
+ *
+ * The browser loads contracts via renderer/js/r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js, an auto-generated
+ * concatenation of contracts/r3.0c/*.js produced by scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js. If a
+ * contract source file is edited but the bundle is not regenerated, the browser sees stale
+ * contract code while Node tests see fresh code — and the divergence would only surface in
+ * actual user behaviour. This test re-runs the generator and asserts the committed bundle is
+ * byte-for-byte equal. CI / pre-merge fails closed on any drift.
+ *
+ * Output: process.exit(1) on any failure; process.exit(0) on success.
+ */
+const fs = require('fs');
+const path = require('path');
+const crypto = require('crypto');
+const Bundle = require('../scripts/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle.js');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function chk(name, cond, detail) { if (cond) { pass++; } else { fail++; console.log('  ✗ ' + name + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + (typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : JSON.stringify(detail)) : '')); } }
+
+const OUT = Bundle.OUT_FILE;
+
+// A. Bundle file exists.
+chk('A1 bundle file exists', fs.existsSync(OUT));
+
+// B. Re-running the generator produces the exact bytes committed.
+const expected = fs.readFileSync(OUT, 'utf8');
+const regenerated = Bundle.buildBundle();
+chk('B1 regenerated length matches committed', regenerated.length === expected.length, { committed: expected.length, regenerated: regenerated.length });
+chk('B2 regenerated content matches committed byte-for-byte', regenerated === expected);
+const expectedHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(expected).digest('hex');
+const regenHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(regenerated).digest('hex');
+chk('B3 regenerated sha256 matches committed sha256', expectedHash === regenHash, { committed: expectedHash, regenerated: regenHash });
+
+// C. Source order honors dependency direction. The bundle must list reason-codes.js BEFORE every
+//    contract that depends on it (which is all of them), and index.js LAST.
+const order = Bundle.SOURCE_ORDER;
+chk('C1 source order starts with reason-codes.js', order[0] === 'reason-codes.js');
+chk('C2 source order ends with index.js', order[order.length - 1] === 'index.js');
+chk('C3 source order includes framing-i18n-key-registry.js', order.indexOf('framing-i18n-key-registry.js') !== -1);
+chk('C4 source order includes viewmodel-state-transition-contract.js', order.indexOf('viewmodel-state-transition-contract.js') !== -1);
+
+// D. Bundle is loadable from a fresh global. We sandbox via the vm module so a Node-side require
+//    (which uses module.exports) is NOT what's tested — we want the BROWSER path. We simulate
+//    that by evaluating the bundle in a context where `module` is undefined.
+const vm = require('vm');
+const sandbox = { console, Buffer };
+sandbox.globalThis = sandbox;
+sandbox.Reflect = Reflect;
+sandbox.Object = Object;
+sandbox.Array = Array;
+sandbox.Number = Number;
+sandbox.Math = Math;
+sandbox.JSON = JSON;
+sandbox.String = String;
+sandbox.Symbol = Symbol;
+sandbox.Error = Error;
+sandbox.WeakSet = WeakSet;
+sandbox.Set = Set;
+sandbox.TextEncoder = TextEncoder;
+vm.createContext(sandbox);
+let evalErr = null;
+try { vm.runInContext(expected, sandbox); } catch (e) { evalErr = e; }
+chk('D1 bundle evaluates cleanly in a browser-like sandbox (no module/exports)', evalErr === null, evalErr && String(evalErr));
+chk('D2 R3_0C_ReasonCodes global registered', typeof sandbox.R3_0C_ReasonCodes === 'object' && sandbox.R3_0C_ReasonCodes !== null);
+chk('D3 R3_0C_FramingI18nKeyRegistry global registered', typeof sandbox.R3_0C_FramingI18nKeyRegistry === 'object' && sandbox.R3_0C_FramingI18nKeyRegistry !== null);
+chk('D4 R3_0C_ViewmodelStateTransitionContract global registered', typeof sandbox.R3_0C_ViewmodelStateTransitionContract === 'object' && sandbox.R3_0C_ViewmodelStateTransitionContract !== null);
+chk('D5 R3_0C_Contracts (aggregator) registered', typeof sandbox.R3_0C_Contracts === 'object' && sandbox.R3_0C_Contracts !== null && typeof sandbox.R3_0C_Contracts.reasonCodes === 'object');
+chk('D6 Contracts.REASON_CODES surfaces the new UI_INITIALIZATION_FAILED code', sandbox.R3_0C_Contracts && sandbox.R3_0C_Contracts.REASON_CODES && sandbox.R3_0C_Contracts.REASON_CODES.UI_INITIALIZATION_FAILED === 'UI_INITIALIZATION_FAILED');
+chk('D7 ALL_REASON_CODES.length === 66', sandbox.R3_0C_ReasonCodes && sandbox.R3_0C_ReasonCodes.ALL_REASON_CODES.length === 66, sandbox.R3_0C_ReasonCodes && sandbox.R3_0C_ReasonCodes.ALL_REASON_CODES.length);
+
+// E. Bundle header carries the auto-generated banner so a human editing the file is warned.
+chk('E1 bundle starts with AUTO-GENERATED banner', /^\/\/ AUTO-GENERATED by scripts\/build-r3-0c-contracts-bundle\.js/.test(expected));
+chk('E2 bundle names the C7-R2-B-01 closure context', expected.indexOf('C7-R2-B-01 closure') !== -1);
+
+console.log('r3-0c-contracts-bundle-equivalence: ' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+if (fail) process.exit(1);

     1	<!DOCTYPE html>
     2	<html lang="zh-TW">
     3	<head>
     4	<meta charset="UTF-8">
     5	<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
     6	<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval'">
     7	<title>Racing Dynamics — Vehicle Dynamics Analyzer</title>
     8	<script src="lib/tailwind.js"></script>
     9	<script defer src="lib/alpine.min.js"></script>
    10	<script src="lib/chart.min.js"></script>
    11	<style>
    12	:root{--bg:#0b1120;--bg2:#111827;--card:#1e293b;--card2:#334155;--accent:#38bdf8;--pink:#f472b6;--green:#34d399;--yellow:#facc15;--red:#ef4444;--text:#e2e8f0;--muted:#94a3b8}
    13	body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--text);font-family:-apple-system,system-ui,sans-serif;margin:0}
    14	.card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06);border-radius:12px}
    15	.card2{background:var(--card2);border-radius:8px}
    16	input,select{background:var(--card2);color:var(--text);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);border-radius:6px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:.85rem}
    17	input:focus,select:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--accent);box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(56,189,248,.2)}
    18	.btn{padding:8px 16px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.15s;font-size:.85rem;border:none}
    19	.btn-primary{background:var(--accent);color:#0f172a}.btn-primary:hover{background:#7dd3fc}
    20	.btn-sm{padding:4px 12px;font-size:.75rem;border-radius:6px}
    21	[x-cloak]{display:none!important}
    22	.nav-btn{padding:12px 16px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;border:1px solid transparent;text-align:center;min-width:80px}
    23	.nav-btn:hover{background:rgba(56,189,248,.08)}
    24	.nav-btn.active{background:rgba(56,189,248,.15);border-color:rgba(56,189,248,.3)}
    25	.badge{display:inline-block;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:.65rem;font-weight:600}
    26	.badge-b{background:rgba(56,189,248,.15);color:#38bdf8}
    27	.badge-p{background:rgba(244,114,182,.15);color:#f472b6}
    28	.badge-g{background:rgba(52,211,153,.15);color:#34d399}
    29	.badge-y{background:rgba(250,204,21,.15);color:#facc15}
    30	.badge-r{background:rgba(239,68,68,.15);color:#ef4444}
    31	table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}
    32	th{text-align:left;padding:6px 10px;color:var(--muted);font-size:.7rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08)}
    33	td{padding:6px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04);font-size:.8rem}
    34	.gauge-bar{height:32px;border-radius:8px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--accent) 0%,var(--green) 35%,var(--yellow) 50%,var(--pink) 65%,var(--red) 100%)}
    35	.gauge-needle{position:absolute;top:0;width:4px;height:100%;background:#fff;border-radius:2px;transition:left .5s ease;box-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(255,255,255,.8)}
    36	.gauge-label{position:absolute;bottom:-18px;font-size:.65rem;color:var(--muted);transform:translateX(-50%)}
    37	.section-title{font-size:1rem;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}
    38	.grid-4{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px}
    39	.grid-2{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:12px}
    40	.corner-box{padding:12px;border-radius:8px;text-align:center;background:var(--card2)}
    41	.corner-box .val{font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:700}
    42	.corner-box .lbl{font-size:.65rem;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px}
    43	.tier-tab{padding:10px 20px;border-radius:8px 8px 0 0;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;font-size:.85rem;transition:.2s;border:1px solid transparent;border-bottom:none}
    44	.tier-tab.active{background:var(--card);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.06)}
    45	.tier-tab:not(.active){background:transparent;color:var(--muted)}
    46	.warn{background:rgba(250,204,21,.08);border:1px solid rgba(250,204,21,.2);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;font-size:.8rem;color:#fde047}
    47	canvas{max-height:300px}
    48	::-webkit-scrollbar{width:5px}::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:rgba(255,255,255,.12);border-radius:3px}
    49	</style>
    50	</head>
    51	<body x-data="app()" x-init="init(); try{document.documentElement.lang=(lang==='zh'?'zh-TW':lang); document.title=t('ui.appTitle')}catch(e){}">
    52	
    53	<!-- Top Nav -->
    54	<header class="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-3 border-b border-white/5" style="background:var(--bg2)">
    55	  <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
    56	    <div class="text-lg font-bold text-sky-400">Racing Dynamics</div>
    57	    <div class="text-xs text-slate-600"><span x-text="t('chrome.subtitle')"></span> v3.4</div>
    58	  </div>
    59	  <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
    60	    <div class="text-xs text-slate-500" x-show="shellSection==='cases' && analysisView" x-cloak>
    61	      <span x-text="t('ui.shell.caseStatusLabel')+' '"></span><span class="text-sky-300 font-semibold" x-text="tCode('ui.status',(caseShellState()&&caseShellState().status)||'')"></span>
    62	    </div>
    63	    <!-- Language switcher -->
    64	    <div class="flex ml-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-white/10">
    65	      <template x-for="lg in langs" :key="lg.id">
    66	        <button class="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold transition-colors"
    67	          :class="lang===lg.id ? 'bg-sky-500 text-white' : 'bg-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'"
    68	          @click="setLang(lg.id)" x-text="lg.label"></button>
    69	      </template>
    70	    </div>
    71	  </div>
    72	</header>
    73	
    74	<!-- R3.0A: Analysis-Case app shell — left main navigation -->
    75	<aside id="shellSidebar" class="fixed left-0 top-[53px] bottom-0 w-52 border-r border-white/5 overflow-y-auto py-3 px-2 z-20" style="background:var(--bg2)">
    76	  <div class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-600 px-3 mb-2" x-text="t('ui.shell.workspace')"></div>
    77	  <template x-for="item in mainNav" :key="item.id">
    78	    <button class="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-lg mb-1 text-sm transition-colors"
    79	      :class="shellSection===item.id ? 'bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-300 border border-sky-500/30' : 'text-slate-400 hover:bg-white/5 border border-transparent'"
    80	      @click="setShellSection(item.id)"><span x-text="t('ui.nav.'+item.id)"></span><span class="float-right text-[10px] text-amber-400/70" x-show="item.deferred" x-cloak x-text="item.deferred"></span></button>
    81	  </template>
    82	  <div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-white/5 text-[11px] text-slate-500 px-2">
    83	    <div x-text="t('ui.shell.status')"></div>
    84	    <div class="text-sky-300 font-semibold" x-text="tCode('ui.status', (caseShellState()&&caseShellState().status)||'empty')"></div>
    85	  </div>
    86	</aside>
    87	
    88	<!-- R3.0A: right fixed Context/Trust panel (Analysis Cases section) -->
    89	<aside id="shellTrustPanel" class="fixed right-0 top-[53px] bottom-0 w-72 border-l border-white/5 overflow-y-auto py-3 px-3 z-20 text-xs" style="background:var(--bg2)" x-show="shellSection==='cases' && analysisView" x-cloak>
    90	  <template x-if="caseShellState()">

   164	                <span class="ml-1 text-[10px]" :class="row.indicators.measured==='measured' ? 'text-violet-300' : 'text-slate-600'" x-text="t('ui.case.ind.meas')+tCode('ui.case.ind.meas', row.indicators.measured)"></span>
   165	                <span class="ml-1 text-[10px]" :class="row.indicators.corner==='eligible' ? 'text-sky-300' : 'text-slate-600'" x-text="t('ui.case.ind.corner')+tCode('ui.case.ind.corner', row.indicators.corner)"></span>
   166	                <span class="ml-1 text-[10px] text-sky-300" x-show="row.caseId===currentCaseId" x-cloak x-text="t('ui.case.row.open')"></span>

  4781	    // Corner circles
  4782	    const corners=[{x:70,y:55,v:cw.fl,l:'FL'},{x:w-70,y:55,v:cw.fr,l:'FR'},{x:70,y:h-55,v:cw.rl,l:'RL'},{x:w-70,y:h-55,v:cw.rr,l:'RR'}];
  4783	    for(const p of corners){
  4784	      const ratio=p.v/maxW;
  4785	      const r=14+ratio*10;
  4786	      const alpha=0.3+ratio*0.5;
  4787	      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(56,189,248,${alpha})`;
  4788	      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  4789	      ctx.fillStyle='#e2e8f0';ctx.font='bold 11px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';
  4790	      ctx.fillText(roundN(p.v,1)+'kg',p.x,p.y+4);
  4791	      ctx.fillStyle='#94a3b8';ctx.font='9px system-ui';
  4792	      ctx.fillText(p.l,p.x,p.y-r-4);
  4793	    }
  4794	    // Info text
  4795	    ctx.fillStyle='#94a3b8';ctx.font='9px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';
  4796	    ctx.fillText(`F/R: ${roundN(cw.front_pct,1)}% / ${roundN(100-cw.front_pct,1)}%`,w/2,h-8);
  4797	    ctx.fillText(`Cross (FL+RR): ${roundN(cw.cross_weight_pct,1)}%`,w/2,18);
  4798	  },
  4799	
  4800	  drawGGDiagram(){
  4801	    if(this.tier!==3||!this.result)return;
  4802	    const c=document.getElementById('ggChart');
  4803	    if(!c)return;
  4804	    if(this.ggChartObj)this.ggChartObj.destroy();
  4805	    // Estimate max lateral G from tire grip
  4806	    const maxLatG=this.result.tire?.front_avg>=0.5?Math.min(this.result.tire.front_avg*1.3,2.0):1.2;
  4807	    const maxBrakeG=this.adv.braking_g>0?this.adv.braking_g:maxLatG*0.9;
  4808	    const maxAccelG=this.adv.accel_g>0?this.adv.accel_g:maxLatG*0.5;
  4809	    // Build friction ellipse points
  4810	    const pts=[];
  4811	    for(let a=0;a<=360;a+=5){
  4812	      const rad=a*Math.PI/180;
  4813	      const latMax=maxLatG;
  4814	      const longMax=Math.sin(rad)>0?maxAccelG:maxBrakeG;
  4815	      const lat=latMax*Math.cos(rad);
  4816	      const lon=longMax*Math.sin(rad);
  4817	      pts.push({x:roundN(lat,3),y:roundN(lon,3)});
  4818	    }
  4819	    this.ggChartObj=new Chart(c,{
  4820	      type:'scatter',
  4821	      data:{datasets:[
  4822	        {label:this.t('ui.chart.frictionCircle'),data:pts,borderColor:'rgba(56,189,248,0.5)',backgroundColor:'rgba(56,189,248,0.05)',fill:true,pointRadius:0,showLine:true,tension:0.1},
  4823	        {label:this.t('ui.chart.origin'),data:[{x:0,y:0}],pointRadius:6,pointBackgroundColor:'#34d399',pointBorderColor:'#34d399'}
  4824	      ]},
  4825	      options:{responsive:true,aspectRatio:1,plugins:{legend:{labels:{color:'#e2e8f0',font:{size:10}}}},
  4826	        scales:{
  4827	          x:{title:{display:true,text:this.t('ui.chart.latG'),color:'#94a3b8'},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8'},min:-maxLatG*1.2,max:maxLatG*1.2},
  4828	          y:{title:{display:true,text:this.t('ui.chart.longG'),color:'#94a3b8'},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8'},min:-maxBrakeG*1.3,max:maxAccelG*1.3}
  4829	        }
  4830	      }
  4831	    });
  4832	  },
  4833	
  4834	  // === Tire Analysis ===
  4835	  getTireParams(id){
  4836	    // 先從 tireOptions (API 資料) 找，否則用 legacy
  4837	    const t = this.tireOptions.find(t=>t.id===id);
  4838	    if(t) return [t.optimal_temp, t.temp_window, t.peak_grip, t.name_zh || t.name];
  4839	    const legacy = {slick_soft:[90,12,1.08],slick_hard:[100,18,1.02],soft:[85,15,1.05],medium:[95,20,1.0],hard:[105,25,.95],wet:[55,30,.7]};
  4840	    const p = legacy[id];
  4841	    return p ? [...p, id] : [95,20,1.0,id];
  4842	  },
  4843	
  4844	  // Phase 2E：把匯入 .tir 既有的曲線資料(fyCurve / muVsLoad)畫出來。不改任何輪胎數學。◈ Model。
  4845	  drawTirCurves(){
  4846	    if(!this.tirResult || this.tirResult.error) return;
  4847	    const axis=(x,y)=>({responsive:true,animation:false,showLine:true,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{x:{title:{display:true,text:x,color:'#94a3b8',font:{size:9}},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8',font:{size:9}}},y:{title:{display:true,text:y,color:'#94a3b8',font:{size:9}},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8',font:{size:9}}}}});
  4848	    const fc=document.getElementById('tirFyChart');
  4849	    if(fc){ if(this.tirFyChartObj)this.tirFyChartObj.destroy();
  4850	      const pts=(this.tirResult.fyCurve||[]).map(p=>({x:p.alpha_deg,y:Math.abs(p.fy_N)}));
  4851	      this.tirFyChartObj=new Chart(fc.getContext('2d'),{type:'scatter',data:{datasets:[{data:pts,borderColor:'#38bdf8',backgroundColor:'rgba(56,189,248,.12)',fill:true,pointRadius:0,tension:.3}]},options:axis(this.t('ui.chart.slipAngleDeg'),this.t('ui.chart.lateralForceN'))});
  4852	    }
  4853	    const mc=document.getElementById('tirMuChart');
  4854	    if(mc){ if(this.tirMuChartObj)this.tirMuChartObj.destroy();
  4855	      const pts=(this.tirResult.muVsLoad||[]).map(p=>({x:Math.round(p.Fz_N/100)/10,y:p.mu}));
  4856	      this.tirMuChartObj=new Chart(mc.getContext('2d'),{type:'scatter',data:{datasets:[{data:pts,borderColor:'#f472b6',backgroundColor:'rgba(244,114,182,.12)',fill:true,pointRadius:0,tension:.3}]},options:axis(this.t('ui.chart.vertLoadKn'),this.t('ui.chart.peakMuAxis'))});
  4857	    }
  4858	  },
  4859	  drawTireGrip(){
  4860	    const ctx=document.getElementById('tireGripChart');if(!ctx)return;
  4861	    if(this.tireGripChartObj)this.tireGripChartObj.destroy();
  4862	    const ds=[];let ci=0;
  4863	    for(const name of this.selCompounds){
  4864	      const[opt,win,peak,label]=this.getTireParams(name);
  4865	      const col=COLORS[ci++%COLORS.length];const pts=[];
  4866	      for(let t=20;t<=140;t+=2){const d=Math.abs(t-opt);const f=Math.exp(-.5*(d/win)**2)*peak;pts.push({x:t,y:Math.round(f*1000)/10})}
  4867	      ds.push({label:label,data:pts,borderColor:col,pointRadius:0,tension:.4});
  4868	    }
  4869	    this.tireGripChartObj=new Chart(ctx,{type:'scatter',data:{datasets:ds},options:{responsive:true,showLine:true,plugins:{legend:{labels:{color:'#e2e8f0',font:{size:10}}}},scales:{x:{title:{display:true,text:this.t('ui.chart.tireTempC'),color:'#94a3b8'},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8'}},y:{title:{display:true,text:this.t('ui.chart.gripAxis'),color:'#94a3b8'},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8'}}}}});
  4870	  },
  4871	  drawTirePress(){
  4872	    const ctx=document.getElementById('tirePressureChart');if(!ctx)return;
  4873	    if(this.tirePressChartObj)this.tirePressChartObj.destroy();
  4874	    const pts=[];for(let p=1.5;p<=2.3;p+=.02){const f=Math.max(50,100-20*Math.abs(p-1.9));pts.push({x:Math.round(p*100)/100,y:Math.round(f*10)/10})}
  4875	    this.tirePressChartObj=new Chart(ctx,{type:'scatter',data:{datasets:[{label:'Grip vs Pressure',data:pts,borderColor:'#38bdf8',backgroundColor:'rgba(56,189,248,.1)',fill:true,pointRadius:0,tension:.4}]},options:{responsive:true,showLine:true,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{x:{title:{display:true,text:this.t('ui.chart.pressureBar'),color:'#94a3b8'},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8'}},y:{title:{display:true,text:this.t('ui.chart.gripAxis'),color:'#94a3b8'},grid:{color:'rgba(255,255,255,.05)'},ticks:{color:'#94a3b8'},min:80,max:110}}}});
  4876	  },
  4877	}}
  4878	</script>
  4879	</body>
  4880	</html>

